08       Canggang Railway Limited    • Annual Report 2025
                                                                      CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
                                                                                董事會主席報告




                                     Liu Yongliang
                                            劉永亮
                                          Chairman
                                        董事會主席




Dear Shareholders,                                                    各位股東：

On behalf of the Board, I hereby present the annual report of the     本 人 謹 代 表 董 事 會 呈 列 本 集 團 截 至2025年12
Group for the year ended 31 December 2025.                            月31日止年度的年度報告。

Overview                                                              概覽
2025 was an important year for the Group to work diligently towards   2025年是本集團沿著既定規劃目標實幹篤行、
its established objectives and make an innovative development.        創新發展的重要一年。一年來，公司管理層堅
Throughout the year, adhering to a “Railway+” strategy-led            持以市場為導向、以效益為中心、以「鐵路+」
and market-oriented approach with efficiency as the core, and         戰略為引領，緊緊圍繞市場開發、降本增效、
centering on three main principles of market development, cost        戰略發展三大主線，積極開拓市場，加快項目
cut and efficiency enhancement and strategic development,             推進，狠抓安全管理，深化改革創新，持續挖
the management of the Company actively expanded markets,              潛增效，搶抓政策機遇，實施裝備升級，保持

STRATEGY
Since the Listing of our shares on the Stock Exchange on 23              自股份於2020年10月23日在聯交所上市以來，
October 2020, the Company has been committed to maintaining              本公司一直致力維持高水平的企業管治。董事
high standard of corporate governance. The Board believes that           會相信，良好的企業管治乃確保所有股東及其
good corporate governance is the basis in ensuring the proper            他持份者的利益皆得到妥善管理的基礎。完善
management in the interest of all our Shareholders and other             企業管治是一項持久且系統性的工程，需要投
stakeholders. Improvement of the corporate governance is a long          入大量精力及毅力。鑒於營商及監管環境不斷
and systematic project, which requires strong commitment and             演變，並為符合持份者的期望，本公司將一如
perseverance. As it always has, the Company will continuously            既往持續完善風險評估和監察流程及內部控
improve the risk assessment and monitoring procedures, internal          制政策，並提升透明度及向股東作出披露的質
control policy, enhancing our transparency and disclosures               素，從而推動本公司可持續及茁壯發展。
quality to our Shareholders to promote the sustainable and
robust development of the Company in light of evolving business
and regulatory environment and to meet the expectations of
stakeholders.

and one-stop solutions of our freight transportation. As a listed        為一間上市公司，我們致力於提供高素質及可
company, we are committed to providing high-quality and                  靠之服務，及通過可持續增長及持續發展來創
reliable services, creating value through sustainable growth and         造價值。
development.

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES                                         企業管治常規

BOARD INDEPENDENCE EVALUATION                                            董事會獨立性評估
During the Reporting Period, the Company has established a board         於報告期內，本公司已制定董事會獨立性評估
independence evaluation mechanism which sets out the processes           機制，列明確保董事會具備充分獨立元素的流
and procedures to ensure a strong independent element on the             程及程序，令董事會得以有效行使獨立判斷，
Board, which allows the Board effectively exercises independent          從而更妥善保障股東權益。
judgment to better safeguard Shareholders’ interests.

The objectives of the evaluation are to improve Board effectiveness,     進 行 評 估 旨 在 提 高 董 事 會 效 率，盡 用 自 身 優
maximise strengths, and identify the areas that need improvement         勢，並識別需再作改進或進一步發展的範疇。
or further development. The evaluation process also clarifies what       評估過程亦可為本公司釐清具體行動以保持並
actions of the Company need to be taken to maintain and improve          提高董事會績效，例如滿足各位董事的個人培
the Board performance, for instance, addressing individual training      訓及發展需求。
and development needs of each Director.

Pursuant to the board independence evaluation mechanism, the             根據董事會獨立性評估機制，董事會對其獨立
board will conduct annual review on its independence. The board          性進行年度審閱。董事會獨立性評估報告會提
independence evaluation report will be presented to the Board            呈董事會，而董事會將集體討論評估結果及改
which will collectively discuss the results and the action plan for      進行動計劃（倘適用）。
improvement, if appropriate.

The Board independence evaluation report was presented to the            呈董事會，評估結果十分理想。

CRISIS MANAGEMENT                                                         危機管理
The rail freight and road freight transportation businesses are           鐵路貨運及道路貨運業務本質上屬危險業務，
inherently dangerous and there are accidents which may result             會發生可能對我們的僱員和其他人士造成致命

are reviewed. IAD is responsible for conducting independent reviews         理及內部監控系統的充足性及有效性進行獨立
of the adequacy and effectiveness of our risk management and                檢討。內審部以就管治、風險管理及內部控制
internal control systems. IAD provides assurance that governance,          （「GRC」）架構及關鍵控制程序乃妥善設計及有

BOARD STATEMENT                                                         董事會聲明
The Board of Directors holds ultimate responsibility for the            董事會對本集團的環境、社會及管治（「環境、
Group’s Environmental, Social and Governance (“ESG”) strategy,          社會及管治」）策略、管治架構、風險管理及匯
governance, risk management and reporting. To ensure effective          報工作承擔最終責任。為確保有效監督，董事
oversight, the Board oversees the process for identifying, assessing,   會負責監督識別、評估、釐定優先次序及管理
prioritising and managing material ESG related issues, including        重大環境、社會及管治相關事宜的流程，當中
climate related risks and opportunities relevant to the Group’s rail    包括有關本集團在中華人民共和國（「中國」）從
freight transportation operations and railway yard management in        事鐵路貨運及鐵路調車場管理業務的氣候相關
the People’s Republic of China (“PRC”).                                 風險和機遇。

As a leading railway operator in Hebei Province leveraging the          作為河北省內利用滄港鐵路線的領先鐵路營運
Canggang Railway Line, the Group recognises that delivering             商，本集團明白，提供可靠的貨運服務及綜合
reliable freight transportation and integrated logistics solutions is   物流解決方案與我們的環境、社會及管治承擔
inextricably linked to our ESG commitments. While we continue to        密不可分。在我們持續鞏固市場地位、探索網
solidify our market position and explore opportunities for network      絡擴展及服務提升機遇的同時，董事會確保可

Identification of Material Topics                                     識別重大議題
In preparing our ESG Report, the Group engaged directly with          編製環境、社會及管治報告時，本集團直接與
various stakeholders to identify and prioritise material topics       各持份者溝通，以識別對我們的經營及持份者
that significantly impact our operations and stakeholders. We         有顯著影響的重大議題，並將其優先排序。我
conducted an online survey through a structured four-step process:    們透過結構化的四步程序進行線上問卷調查：
identification, prioritisation, verification, and review.             鑒別、排序、確證和檢視。

This process led to the identification of five key material topics    透過該程序，我們識別了與營運密切相關的五
closely linked to our operations: “Environmental Protection,          項 關 鍵 重 大 議 題：「環 保、節 能 與 減 排」、「運
Energy Saving and Emission Reduction”, “Transportation Safety”,       輸安全」、「人力資本」、「供應鏈管理」及「社區
“Human Capital”, “Supply Chain Management”, and “Community            投入」。該等議題反映我們對可持續發展的承
Investment”. These topics reflect our commitment to sustainability    諾，且致力與持份者培養正面的關係。
and our dedication to fostering positive stakeholder relationships.

1.   Identification                                                   1.   鑒別
     Commissioned an independent third-party consultancy to                委託獨立第三方顧問公司，透過參考聯交
     conduct materiality assessment of the Group with reference to         所守則對本集團進行重要性評估。
     the Code issued by the Stock Exchange.

2.   Prioritization                                                   2.   排序
     Invited stakeholders and the management of the Group to               邀請各持份者及本集團管理層參與線上問
     participate in an online survey to gauge their views on the           卷調查，收集其對潛在重大議題重要性的
     significance of potential material topics. Prioritized and            意見。根據「經濟、環境及社會影響的重
     confirmed the material topics based on two dimensions, namely         要性」及「對持份者評估及決策的影響」兩
     “significance of economic, environmental and social impacts”          個層面，進行排序並確認重大議題。
     and “influence on stakeholders’ assessments and decision
     making”.

3.   Verification                                                     3.   確認
     The management of the Group closely monitored and                     本集團管理層密切審視並確認重要性評估
     confirmed the assessment results of material topics, and they         結果，並將其披露於本報告中。
     will be disclosed in this report.

Governance                                                          管理
To effectively address climate risks and opportunities, we have     為有效應對氣候風險及機遇，我們已將氣候相
fully integrated climate initiatives into the Group’s broader ESG   關措施全面納入本集團整體環境、社會及管治
governance framework. We operate a two tier governance system       管理架構。本集團實行雙層管理機制，確保由
that ensures clear top down accountability and aligns seamlessly    上至下責任清晰明確，並與整體可持續發展管
with our overall sustainability management structure.               理架構緊密對接。

 Decision Making Level: The Board      The Board serves as the highest decision making body for ESG
 決策層：董事會                               matters and assumes ultimate accountability for the Group’s
                                       climate strategies, policies and risk management. Its core
                                       responsibilities include:
                                       董事會為環境、社會及管治事宜的最高決策機構，就本集團的氣候相關
                                       策略、政策及風險管理承擔最終責任。其核心職責包括：

                                       ■   Approving and formulating climate strategies, policies, annual
                                           targets and major resource allocation plans.
                                       ■   審批及制訂氣候相關策略、政策、年度目標及主要資源分配方案。

                                       ■   Overseeing the identification, assessment and management
                                           effectiveness of climate risks and opportunities, ensuring
                                           alignment with the overall business strategy.
                                       ■   監督氣候風險與機遇的識別、評估及管理成效，確保有關工作與整體業
                                           務策略保持一致。

                                       ■   Coordinating cross departmental climate action and resolving
                                           significant implementation barriers.
                                       ■   協調跨部門氣候行動，並解決執行過程中的重大障礙。

 Management Level: The ESG             Acting as the central management hub for climate initiatives,
 Working Group                         the ESG Working Group bridges the Board and our operational
 管理層：環境、社會及管治工作小組                      teams. Its core responsibilities include:
                                       環境、社會及管治工作小組為氣候相關措施的核心管理樞紐，擔當董事
                                       會與營運團隊之間的橋樑。其核心職責包括：

                                       ■   Tracking the progress of core issues such as carbon emission
                                           management, energy efficient technology development and the
                                           low carbon transformation of our supply chain.
                                       ■   跟進碳排放管理、節能技術研發及供應鏈低碳轉型等核心議題的進度。

                                       ■   Assisting in the formulation of climate implementation guidelines,
                                           targets and assessment metrics.
                                       ■   協助制訂氣候相關執行指引、目標及評估指標。

                                       ■   Reporting regularly to the Board on work performance,
                                           implementation challenges and significant matters requiring
                                           higher level decision making.
                                       ■   定期向董事會匯報工作成效、執行挑戰及須上報高層決策的重大事項。

Strategy                                                                   策略
The Group regards climate change as a key factor affecting its             本集團將氣候變化視為影響其長期業務發展及
long term business development and financial performance. We               財務表現的關鍵因素。我們有系統地識別及評
systematically identify and assess transition risks and physical risks,    估轉型風險與物理風險，分析其對本集團收入
analysing their potential impact on the Group’s revenue structure,         結構、營運開支及資本開支的潛在影響。現行
operating costs and capital expenditures. Our assessments are              評估分三個時間層級開展：短期（1至5年）、中
currently conducted across three time horizons: short term (1-5            期（6至10年）及長期（超過10年），有關劃分與
years), medium term (6-10 years) and long term (more than 10               本集團內部業務規劃緊密對接。
years), which are closely aligned with our internal business planning.

We are progressively adjusting our business and operational                我 們 正 逐 步 調 整 業 務 及 營 運 策 略，以 應 對 氣
strategies to address climate challenges. To mitigate physical             候 相 關 挑 戰。為 減 輕 極 端 天 氣 及 熱 浪 等 物 理
risks such as extreme weather and heatwaves, we continuously               風險，我們持續升級鐵路軌道及設施，同時嚴
upgrade our railway tracks and facilities while strictly implementing      格執行安全規範，包括縮短戶外工作時數，並
safety protocols, including shortening outdoor working hours and           撥 出 專 項 預 算 購 置 防 暑 設 備，以 保 障 前 線 員
allocating specific budgets for heatstroke prevention equipment            工安全。為使核心鐵路營運業務有效管理轉型
to protect our frontline staff. For our core rail operations to            風險，我們優先採用節能機車，並持續升級貨
manage transition risks, we prioritize the adoption of energy-             場，以減少電力及柴油消耗。為確保我們的戰
efficient locomotives and the continuous upgrading of our freight          略方向始終走在時代前沿，我們密切關注本地
yards to reduce electricity and diesel fuel consumption. To ensure         及國際可持續發展報告指引以及中國「十五五」
our strategic direction remains ahead of the curve, we closely             規劃的最新動態，積極調整我們的可持續發展
monitor local and international sustainability reporting guidelines,       政策，以符合國家要求。
as well as developments in the national “15th Five-Year Plan”,
proactively adjusting our sustainability policies to align with national
requirements.

Furthermore, we are actively exploring market opportunities                此外，我們正積極挖掘與綠色物流及低碳運輸
related to green logistics and low carbon transportation services.         服務相關的市場機遇。藉著國家「公轉鐵」運輸
By capitalising on the national “Road to Rail” transportation shift,       結構調整政策，我們致力於把握客戶對可持續
we aim to capture the growing customer demand for sustainable              供應鏈日益增長的需求。作為我們提升綠色服
supply chains. As part of our commitment to enhancing our green            務能力承諾的一部分，本集團已做好準備，於
service capabilities, the Group is prepared to obtain relevant             未來因應客戶的具體需求取得相關環境證書。
environmental certificates in the future in response to specific           透過提供環保鐵路貨運解決方案，我們正將氣
customer requirements. By offering environmentally friendly rail           候相關機遇轉化為本集團業務增長的新動力。
freight solutions, we are transforming climate opportunities into new
drivers of business growth for the Group.

Sewage Discharge                                                            污水排放

Environmental Policies and Performance                                 環境政策及表現
We are subject to PRC national and local laws and regulations          我們須遵守中國國家和地方有關環境保護、污
relating to environmental protection, prevention and control of        染防治和事故報告的法律及法規。我們已向相
pollution, and incident reporting. We have obtained all pollutant      關地方環保部門取得所有必要污染物排放許可
discharge permits necessary from the relevant local environmental      證。
protection authority.

We regard environmental protection as an important corporate           我們將環境保護作為一項重要的企業責任，致
responsibility and are dedicated to implementing a comprehensive       力於在日常營運中實施全面的環境保護管理體
environmental protection management system in our daily                系。我們的安全監控辦公室負責制定和實施有
operations. Our safety supervision office is responsible for           關環境保護的內部規則和法規。我們已在鐵路
developing and implementing internal rules and regulations on          和貨場等各個地點採納工作程序規定，以確保
environmental protection. We have adopted regulations in respect       遵守環境保護法律及法規。
of working procedure at various locations including railways and
freight yards to ensure compliance with environmental protection
laws and regulations.

Compliance with Laws and Regulations                                   遵守法律及法規
The Group is subject to various laws and regulations including,        本集團須遵守中國有關我們所在行業的多項法

1 CORPORATE INFORMATION                                              1 公司資料
  Canggang Railway Limited (the “Company”) was incorporated           滄 港 鐵 路 有 限 公 司（「本 公 司」）為 於2018
  in the Cayman Islands on 19 October 2018 as an exempted             年10月19日 根 據 開 曼 群 島 公 司 法 第22章
  company with limited liability under the Companies Law,             （1961年第3號法例，經綜合及修訂）在開
  Cap. 22 (Law 3 of 1961, as consolidated and revised) of the         曼群島註冊成立的獲豁免有限公司。本公
  Cayman Islands. The shares of the Company were listed on            司 股 份 於2020年10月23日 在 香 港 聯 合 交
  the Main Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited           易所有限公司（「聯交所」）主板上市。本公
  (the “Stock Exchange”) on 23 October 2020. The Company              司及其附屬公司的主要業務為透過在中華
  and its subsidiaries are principally engaged in the provision       人民共和國（「中國」）經營貨運鐵路及鐵路
  of rail freight transportation and related ancillary services by    調車場提供鐵路貨運及相關輔助服務。
  operating freight railway and railway yards in the People’s
  Republic of China (the “PRC”).

2 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                       2 重大會計政策
  (a) Statement of compliance                                         (a) 合規聲明

     (e) Associates                                                      (e) 聯營公司

          An associate is an entity in which the group or the              聯營公司指本集團或本公司可以對其
          company has significant influence, but not control or joint      發揮重大影響力但並非控制或共同控
          control, over the financial and operating policies.              制其財務及經營政策的實體。

          An interest in an associate is accounted for using the           於聯營公司的權益以權益法入賬，除
          equity method, unless it is classified as held for sale (or      非分類為持作出售（或計入分類為持
          included in a disposal group classified as held for sale).       作出售的出售集團）。其最初以成本
          They are initially recognised at cost, which includes            確認，包括交易成本。隨後，綜合財
          transaction costs. Subsequently, the consolidated financial      務報表包括本集團分佔該等被投資對
          statements include the group’s share of the profit or            象的損益及其他全面收益（「其他全面
          loss and other comprehensive income (“OCI”) of those             收益」），直至失去重大影響力之日。
          investees, until the date on which significant influence
          ceases.

          When the group’s share of losses exceeds its interest            當集團所佔的虧損超過其於該聯營
          in the associate, the group’s interest is reduced to             公 司 的 權 益 時，集 團 的 權 益 會 減 至
          nil and recognition of further losses is discontinued            零，並且不再確認進一步虧損，惟集
          except to the extent that the group has incurred legal           團須履行法定或推定義務，或代被投
          or constructive obligations or made payments on behalf           資對象作出付款則除外。就此而言，
          of the investee. For this purpose, the group’s interest is       集團的權益乃按權益法得出的投資賬
          the carrying amount of the investment under the equity           面值，連同實質上組成集團於該聯營
          method, together with any other long-term interests that in      公司的淨投資的任何其他長期權益
          substance form part of the group’s net investment in the         （就有關其他長期權益應用預期信貸
          associate, after applying the expected credit loss (“ECL”)       虧損模式（「信貸虧損模式」）後（如適
          model to such other long-term interests where applicable.        用））。

          Unrealised gains arising from transactions with equity-          與按權益法入賬被投資對象進行交易
          accounted investees are eliminated against the investment        所產生的未變現收益與投資對銷，惟

           the ECL assessment.                                                      期信貸虧損評估。

           Measurement of ECLs                                                      預期信貸虧損計量

           ECLs are a probability-weighted estimate of credit losses.               預期信貸虧損乃以概率加權估計的信
           Generally, credit losses are measured as the present value               貸虧損。一般而言，信貸虧損計量為
           of all expected cash shortfalls between the contractual and              合約金額及預期金額之間的所有預期
           expected amounts.                                                        現金短欠額之現值。

           The expected cash shortfalls are discounted using the                    倘貼現影響屬重大，預期現金短欠額
           following discount rates where the effect of discounting is              將使用以下貼現率貼現：
           material:

           –    fixed-rate financial assets, trade and bills receivables,           －   固定利率金融資產、貿易應收款
                other receivables and contract assets: effective                        項及應收票據、其他應收款項及
                interest rate determined at initial recognition or an                   合約資產：初始確認時釐定的實
                approximation thereof;                                                  際利率或其近似值；

           –    variable-rate financial assets: current effective interest          －   浮動利率金融資產：當前實際利
                rate.                                                                   率。

           The maximum period considered when estimating ECLs is                    於估計預期信貸虧損時考慮的最長期
           the maximum contractual period over which the Group is                   間為本集團承受信貸風險的最長合約
           exposed to credit risk.                                                  期間。

           ECLs are measured on either of the following bases:                      預期信貸虧損基於下列其中一項基準
                                                                                    計量：

           –    12-month ECLs: these are these are the portion of                   －   12個月預期信貸虧損：於報告日
                ECLs that result from default events that are possible                  期後12個月（或倘有關工具的預
                within the 12 months after the reporting date (or a                     期 年 限 少 於12個 月，則 一 段 較
                shorter period if the expected life of the instrument is                短期間）內可能發生的違約事件
                less than 12 months); and                                               所導致的預期信貸虧損部分；及

           –    lifetime ECLs: these are the ECLs that result from all              －   全期預期信貸虧損：於預期信貸
                possible default events over the expected lives of the                  虧損模型所適用項目的預期年限
                items to which the ECL model applies.                                   內可能發生的所有違約事件所導
                                                                                        致的預期信貸虧損。

        Significant increases in credit risk                                   信貸風險顯著增加

        When determining whether the credit risk of a financial                於釐定金融工具的信貸風險自初始確
        instrument has increased significantly since initial                   認以來有否顯著增加，以及於計量預
        recognition and when measuring ECLs, the Group                         期信貸虧損時，本集團會考慮與之相
        considers reasonable and supportable information that is               關的合理可靠資料，且有關資料毋須
        relevant and available without undue cost or effort. This              付出過多成本或努力即可獲得。這包
        includes both quantitative and qualitative information and             括基於本集團的過往經驗及知情信貸
        analysis, based on the Group’s historical experience and               評估的定量及定性資料及分析，當中
        informed credit assessment, that includes forward-looking              包含前瞻性資料。
        information.

        In particular, the following information is taken into                 具體而言，於評估信貸風險自初始確

        significantly since initial recognition:                               料：

        –   failure to make payments of principal or interest on               －   未能於合約到期日支付本金或利
            their contractually due dates;                                         息；

            financial instrument’s external or internal credit rating              （倘適用）實際或預期明顯轉差；
            (if available);

            operating results of the debtor; and                                   顯轉差；及

            market, economic or legal environment that have a                      現有變化或預期變化對債務人履
            significant adverse effect on the debtor’s ability to                  行對本集團責任的能力造成重大
            meet its obligation to the Group.                                      不利影響。

        Write-off policy                                                          撇銷政策

        The gross carrying amount of a financial asset or contract                倘實際上並無收回款項的可能，則金
        asset is written off (either partially or in full) to the extent          融資產或合約資產的賬面總值會予撇
        that there is no realistic prospect of recovery. This is                  銷（部分或全部）。此情況通常出現於
        generally the case when the Group determines that the                     本集團確定債務人並無資產或可產生
        debtor does not have assets or sources of income that                     足夠現金流量的收入來源以償還應予
        could generate sufficient cash flows to repay the amounts                 撇銷的金額之時。
        subject to the write-off.

        Subsequent recoveries of an asset that was previously                     其後收回先前被撇銷的資產於收回期
        written off are recognised as a reversal of impairment in                 間在損益中確認為減值撥回。
        profit or loss in the period in which the recovery occurs.

  (j)   Impairment of other non-current assets                              (j)   其他非流動資產減值

        At each reporting date, the Group reviews the carrying                    於各報告日期，本集團會審閱其非金
        amounts of its non-financial assets (other than property                  融資產（除按重估金額計值之物業、
        carried at revalued amounts, investment property,                         投 資 物 業、存 貨 及 其 他 合 約 成 本、
        inventories and other contract costs, contract assets and                 合約資產及遞延稅項資產外）之賬面
        deferred tax assets) to determine whether there is any                    值，以釐定有否減值跡象。倘出現任
        indication of impairment. If any such indication exists, then             何有關跡象，則會對該資產的可收回
        the asset’s recoverable amount is estimated. Goodwill is                  金額作出估計。每年均會對商譽進行
        tested annually for impairment.                                           減值測試。

        For impairment testing, assets are grouped together into                  就減值測試而言，資產獲組合成為能
        the smallest group of assets that generates cash inflows                  夠在持續使用中產生現金流入，且其
        from continuing use that are largely independent of the                   產生之現金流入能大致區別於其他資
        cash inflows of other assets or cash-generating units                     產或現金產生單位（「現金產生單位」）
        (“CGU”s). Goodwill arising from a business combination is                 之最小資產組別。由業務合併產生之
        allocated to CGUs or groups of CGUs that are expected to                  商譽獲分配至預期可受惠於有關合併
        benefit from the synergies of the combination.                            之協同效應之一組或多組現金產生單
                                                                                  位。

        The recoverable amount of an asset or CGU is the greater                  資產或現金產生單位的可收回金額為
        of its value in use and its fair value less costs of disposal.            其使用價值與公平值減出售成本兩者
        Value in use is based on the estimated future cash flows,                 的較高者。使用價值乃基於估計未來
        discounted to their present value using a pre-tax discount                現金流量釐定，並按反映當前市場對
        rate that reflects current market assessments of the time                 貨幣時間價值及該資產或現金產生單
        value of money and the risks specific to the asset or CGU.                位特定風險評估的稅前貼現率貼現至
                                                                                  其現值。

        An impairment loss is recognised if the carrying amount of                當資產或現金產生單位的賬面值高
        an asset or CGU exceeds its recoverable amount.                           於其可收回金額時，則會確認減值虧
                                                                                  損。

        Impairment losses are recognised in profit or loss. They                  減值虧損於損益內確認。分配減值虧
        are allocated first to reduce the carrying amount of any                  損乃首先用以減低已分配至現金產生
        goodwill allocated to the CGU, and then to reduce the                     單位之任何商譽的賬面值，其後用以
        carrying amounts of the other assets in the CGU on a pro                  按比例減低該現金產生單位的其他資
        rata basis.                                                               產的賬面值。

     (p) Interest-bearing borrowings (continued)                           (p) 計息借款（續）

           The Group entered into the arrangements with the                      本 集 團 與 融 資 租 賃 機 構 訂 有 安 排，
           financial leasing institutions in which the Group sold                據此，本集團向該等機構出售若干設
           certain equipment to these institutions and then leased               備，其後再按預定租賃付款租回。本
           back with scheduled lease payments. The Group has                     集團可選擇於租賃期結束時以象徵性
           an option to buy back these equipment at a token price                價格購回該等設備，即議價收購選擇
           at the end of the lease term, i.e. the bargain purchase               權。考慮到議價收購選擇權按象徵性
           option. Considering that the bargain purchase option is               價格定價，故幾乎可以肯定本集團將
           set at a token price, it is almost certain that the Group             會行使有關選擇權。有關安排實質上

     (s)   Provisions and contingent liabilities                              (s)   撥備及或然負債

           Generally provisions are determined by discounting the                   一般而言，撥備乃按反映當前市場對
           expected future cash flows at a pre-tax rate that reflects               貨幣時間價值及對該負債特定風險評

           the risks specific to the liability.                                     而釐定。

           A provision for warranties is recognised when the                        保修撥備於出售相關產品或服務時確
           underlying products or services are sold, based on                       認，其乃按過往保修數據及可能的結
           historical warranty data and a weighting of possible                     果與其相關概率的加權而定。
           outcomes against their associated probabilities.

           A provision for onerous contracts is measured at the                     虧損性合約撥備乃按終止合約的預期
           present value of the lower of the expected cost of                       成本及繼續履行合約的成本淨額（有
           terminating the contract and the expected net cost of                    關金額乃按照履行合約項下的責任之
           continuing with the contract, which is determined based on               增量成本及與履行該合約直接相關之
           the incremental costs of fulfilling the obligation under that            其 他 成 本 分 配 而 釐 定）
                                                                                                     （以 較 低 者 為
           contract and an allocation of other costs directly related               準）的現值計量。在計提撥備前，本
           to fulfilling that contract. Before a provision is established,          集團就該合約的相關資產確認任何減
           the Group recognises any impairment loss on the assets                   值虧損。
           associated with that contract.

           Where it is not probable that an outflow of economic                     倘不大可能需要流出經濟利益或未能
           benefits will be required, or the amount cannot be                       可靠估計金額，有關責任會披露為或
           estimated reliably, the obligation is disclosed as a                     然負債，除非流出經濟利益的可能性
           contingent liability, unless the probability of outflow of               極微，則作別論。僅由於一項或多項
           economic benefits is remote. Possible obligations, whose                 未來事項發生或不發生而確認是否存
           existence will only be confirmed by the occurrence or                    在的可能責任亦會披露為或然負債，
           non-occurrence of one or more future events are also                     惟流出經濟利益的可能性極低者則除
           disclosed as contingent liabilities unless the probability of            外。
           outflow of economic benefits is remote.

        the exchange rate at the reporting date. Non-monetary                外幣計並按公平值計量的非貨幣資產

        in a foreign currency are translated into the functional             算為功能貨幣。以外幣計並按歷史成

        measured based on historical cost in a foreign currency              般於損益確認。
        are translated at the exchange rate at the date of the
        transaction. Foreign currency differences are generally
        recognised in profit or loss.

        The assets and liabilities of foreign operations, are                海外經營業務的資產及負債乃按於報

             Islands.

             regulations, the Company’s subsidiaries established                 司於中國內地成立及營運的附屬
             and operated in Mainland China are required to                      公 司 須 將 有 關 公 司 的10%純 利
             transfer 10% of their net profit to the statutory                   轉撥至法定儲備，直至儲備結餘
             reserve until the reserve balance reaches 50% of                    達 到 各 自 註 冊 資 本 的50%。向
             the respective registered capital. The transfer to                  該儲備作出的轉撥須於向權益持
             this reserve must be made before distributions to                   有人作出分派前作出。有關儲備
             equity holders. This reserve can be utilized in setting             可用於抵銷累計虧損或附屬公司
             off accumulated losses or increase capital of the                   增資，且除清盤外，不可用於分
             subsidiaries and is non-distributable other than in                 派。
             liquidation.

      (iv) Safety production fund reserve                                 (iv) 安全生產基金儲備

             The Group is engaged in freight transportation                      本集團從事貨運業務。根據中國
             business. In accordance with the regulations issued                 財政部及國家安全生產監督管
             by Ministry of Finance and State Administration of                  理總局頒佈的規定，本集團須設
             Work Safety of the PRC, the Group is required to                    立專項儲備（即安全生產基金儲
             establish a special reserve namely safety production                備），有關儲備乃按上一個年度
             fund reserve, which is calculated at 1% of the regular              一般貨運收益的1%及危險品貨
             freight transportation revenue and 1.5% of the                      運 收 益 的1.5%計 算。安 全 生 產
             dangerous goods freight transportation revenue of                   基金儲備主要用於購置及維護
             the previous year. The safety production fund reserve               安全設備及設施。就財務報表而
             is mainly used for the acquisitions and maintenance                 言，有關儲備乃按上述方法透過
             of safety equipment and facilities. For the purpose                 自保留盈利撥款而設立，並在產
             of financial statements, such reserve is established                生相關開支時動用。
             through an appropriation from retained earnings

          (v)   Exchange reserve                                              (v)   匯兌儲備

                The exchange reserve comprises all foreign exchange                 匯兌儲備包括因將本集團旗下公
                difference arising from the translation of the financial            司的財務報表換算為本集團呈列
                statements of the companies comprising the Group                    貨幣而產生的所有匯兌差額。有
                into the Group’s presentation currency. The reserve is              關儲備乃按照附註2(u)所載的會
                dealt with in accordance with the accounting policies               計政策處理。
                set out in Note 2(u).

     (e) Capital management                                                 (e) 資本管理

          The Group’s primary objectives when managing capital                本集團管理資本的主要目標為保障本
          are to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a               集團持續經營業務的能力，使其可透
          going concern, so that it can continue to provide returns           過使產品及服務的定價與風險水平一
          for shareholders and benefits for other stakeholders,               致及按合理成本取得融資，以繼續為
          by pricing products and services commensurately with                股東帶來回報並惠及其他持份者。
          the level of risk and by securing access to finance at a
          reasonable cost.

          The Group actively and regularly reviews and manages                本集團積極及定期檢討及管理其資本
          its capital structure to maintain a balance between the             架構，以在較高股東回報（可能伴隨
          higher shareholders returns that might be possible with             較高借貸水平）與穩健的資本狀況所
          higher levels of borrowings and the advantages and                  帶來的優勢及保障之間取得平衡，並
          security afforded by a sound capital position, and makes            依照經濟狀況的變化對資本架構作出
          adjustments to the capital structure in light of changes in         調整。
          economic conditions.

          Neither the Company nor any of its subsidiaries are subject         本公司或其任何附屬公司概無受任何

     Exposure to credit, liquidity and interest rate risks arises in the     本集團在日常業務過程中面對信貸、流動
     normal course of the Group’s business.                                  資金及利率風險。

     The Group’s exposure to these risks and the financial risk              本集團所面對的該等風險及本集團用以管
     management policies and practices used by the Group to                  理該等風險的金融風險管理政策和慣例載
     manage these risks are described below.                                 述如下。

     (a) Credit risk                                                         (a) 信貸風險

          Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will default      信貸風險指對手方不履行合約責任導
          on its contractual obligations resulting in a financial loss to      致本集團蒙受財務損失的風險。本集
          the Group. The Group’s credit risk is primarily attributable         團的信貸風險主要來自貿易應收款
          to trade receivables. The Group’s exposure to credit                 項。本集團就現金及現金等值項目和
          risk arising from cash and cash equivalents and bills                應收票據所承受的信貸風險有限，原
          receivable is limited because the counterparties are banks           因是對手方為本集團管理層給予高信
          and financial institutions with high credit ratings assigned         貸評級的銀行及金融機構，故本集團
          by the management of the Group, for which the Group                  認為其信貸風險偏低。
          considers to have low credit risk.

          The Group does not provide any guarantees which would                本集團不會提供將令本集團面對信貸
          expose the Group to credit risk.                                     風險的任何擔保。

          Trade receivables                                                    貿易應收款項

          The Group’s exposure to credit risk is influenced mainly by          本集團面對的信貸風險主要受各客戶
          the individual characteristics of each customer rather than          （而非客戶經營所在的行業或國家）的
          the industry or country in which the customers operate               個別情況所影響，因此，重大集中信
          and therefore significant concentrations of credit risk              貸風險主要於本集團與個別客戶有重
          primarily arise when the Group has significant exposure to           大業務往來時產生。於報告期末，貿
          individual customers. At the end of the reporting period,            易應收款項總額中的30%（2024年：
          30% (2024: 44%) of the total trade receivables was due               44%）乃來自本集團的最大客戶，而
          from the Group’s largest customer and 77% (2024: 70%)                貿 易 應 收 款 項 總 額 中 的77%（ 2024
          of the total trade receivables was due from the five largest         年：70%）乃來自五大客戶。
          customers.

          Individual credit evaluations are performed on all                   本集團會對所有要求超過一定金額信
          customers requiring credit over a certain amount. These              貸的客戶進行個別信貸評估。該等評
          evaluations focus on the customer’s past history of making           估集中於客戶過往支付到期款項的記
          payments when due and current ability to pay, and take               錄及現時的付款能力，並考慮客戶及
          into account information specific to the customer as well            與客戶經營所在地的經濟環境有關的
          as pertaining to the economic environment in which the               特定資料。貿易應收款項於開票日期
          customer operates. Trade receivables are due within 30               起計30天內到期。本集團一般不向客
          days from the date of billing. Normally, the Group does not          戶收取抵押品。
          obtain collateral from customers.
